<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warmhearted</td><td>/ˈwɔrm ˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热心的，热情友善的</td><td>a warmhearted neighbor<br>一位热心的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their warmhearted host insisted that everyone stay for dinner.  他们热心的主人坚持要所有人留下吃饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>callow</b></td><td>/ˈkæloʊ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不老练的，不成熟的</td><td>a callow youth<br>不成熟的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The callow recruit asked questions that made the veterans laugh.  这名稚嫩的新兵问的问题让老兵们发笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 green</td><td>/ɡriːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无经验的</td><td>green recruits<br>毫无经验的新兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The green interns needed close supervision during their very first month.  这些毫无经验的实习生在最初一个月需要密切的指导。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immature</td><td>/ˌɪməˈtʃʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（生理、心理）未完全发展的，未发育成熟的</td><td>emotionally immature<br>情绪上不成熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fruit is still immature and much too sour to pick.  这些果子还未成熟，太酸，不能采摘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inexperienced</td><td>/ˌɪnɪkˈspɪriənst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏经验的，不熟练的</td><td>an inexperienced driver<br>缺乏经验的司机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team was young and inexperienced but full of energy.  这支队伍年轻缺乏经验，却充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 juvenile</td><td>/ˈdʒuːvənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>少年的；幼稚的；青少年</td><td>juvenile behavior<br>幼稚的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His juvenile jokes embarrassed everyone at the formal dinner.  他幼稚的玩笑让正式晚宴上的人都很尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unfledged</del></td><td>/ʌnˈflɛdʒd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>羽毛未齐的；未成熟的</td><td>an unfledged lawyer<br>尚不老练的律师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unfledged birds could not yet leave the nest.  这些羽毛未齐的雏鸟还不能离巢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unripened</del></td><td>/ʌnˈraɪpənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未成熟的，未熟透的</td><td>unripened green fruit<br>未熟的青果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unripened peaches tasted hard and unpleasantly sour.  这些没熟的桃子又硬又酸，很不好吃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 puerile</td><td>/ˈpjʊrəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>幼稚的，不成熟的</td><td>puerile humor<br>幼稚的幽默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His puerile complaints annoyed the whole committee.  他幼稚的抱怨让整个委员会都不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adult</td><td>/əˈdʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>成年的，成熟的；成年人</td><td>an adult attitude<br>成熟的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the crisis in a calm, adult way.  她以冷静成熟的方式处理了这场危机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 experienced</td><td>/ɪkˈspɪriənst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有经验的，老练的</td><td>an experienced guide<br>经验丰富的导游</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We hired an experienced nurse to care for my grandmother.  我们请了一位有经验的护士照顾我祖母。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grown-up</td><td>/ˈɡroʊnʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>成年的；成年人</td><td>act like a grown-up<br>像成年人一样行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the grown-ups were allowed to stay for the discussion.  只有成年人被允许留下参加讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mature</td><td>/məˈtʃʊr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>成熟的；（使）成熟</td><td>a mature judgment<br>成熟的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seems remarkably mature for a boy of fifteen.  对一个十五岁男孩来说他显得格外成熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ripe</td><td>/ˈraɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>成熟的，熟透的；时机成熟的</td><td>ripe for change<br>变革时机成熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaches are finally ripe enough to pick and eat.  这些桃子终于熟到可以采下来吃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>calumniate</b></del></td><td>/kəˈlʌmnɪeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>诽谤，造谣，中伤</td><td>calumniate a rival<br>诽谤竞争对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents calumniated the mayor with stories no one could prove.  反对者用无人能证实的说法诽谤市长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 asperse</del></td><td>/əˈspɜrs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>asperse his character<br>中伤他的品格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet aspersed every officer who supported the reform.  这本小册子中伤了每一位支持改革的官员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blacken</td><td>/ˈblækən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变黑；诽谤，玷污</td><td>blacken his reputation<br>玷污他的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors quickly blackened the name of the honest official.  谣言很快玷污了这位清廉官员的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defame</td><td>/dɪˈfeɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>defame a public figure<br>诽谤公众人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sued the paper for trying to defame him.  他起诉那家报纸企图诽谤他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 libel</td><td>/ˈlaɪbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>书面诽谤；（以文字）诽谤</td><td>sue for libel<br>以诽谤罪起诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actor claimed the article libeled him and his family.  这位演员称该文诽谤了他和他的家人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malign</td><td>/məˈlaɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>恶意的；诽谤</td><td>malign his motives<br>诋毁他的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rival newspapers began to malign the senator without offering a shred of evidence.  竞争对手的报纸开始诽谤这位参议员，却拿不出一丝证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smear</td><td>/ˈsmɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>诽谤，抹黑；涂抹</td><td>a smear campaign<br>抹黑运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rivals tried to smear the candidate with unfounded financial rumors.  对手试图用毫无根据的财务谣言抹黑这位候选人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 traduce</del></td><td>/trəˈduːs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>traduce an opponent<br>中伤对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rival papers traduced the senator throughout the long campaign.  对立的报纸在整场竞选中中伤这位参议员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vilify</td><td>/ˈvɪlɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，辱骂</td><td>vilify the whistleblower<br>诽谤举报者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper tried to vilify the whistleblower instead of examining her evidence.  报纸试图诽谤举报者，而不是审查她的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vindicate</td><td>/ˈvɪndɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…平反，为…辩护，使无罪；证明，证实</td><td>vindicate one's reputation<br>为自己的名誉洗冤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results vindicated her unusual approach.  结果证明了她那不寻常的方法是对的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flattering</td><td>/ˈflætərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奉承的；美化的</td><td>a flattering description<br>奉承美化的描述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The portrait was flattering but hardly looked like him.  这幅画像很美化，却几乎不像他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 approbation</td><td>/ˌæprəˈbeɪʃn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞许，认可，同意</td><td>win official approbation<br>获得官方认可</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan finally won the approbation of the whole city council.  该计划最终获得了整个市议会的同意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>camaraderie</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːməˈrɑːdəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>同伴情谊，友情</td><td>a sense of camaraderie<br>同伴间的情谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strong camaraderie grew among the players during the long season.  漫长的赛季让球员之间生出深厚的情谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brotherhood</td><td>/ˈbrʌðərhʊd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>兄弟情谊；同业团体</td><td>a spirit of brotherhood<br>兄弟般的情谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long marches created a deep brotherhood among the soldiers.  长途行军在士兵之间造就了深厚情谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comradeship</td><td>/ˈkɑːmrædʃɪp/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>同志情谊，伙伴之情</td><td>wartime comradeship<br>战时的同志情谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They remembered the comradeship of those difficult early years.  他们怀念那些艰难早年的伙伴情谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fellowship</td><td>/ˈfeloʊˌʃɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友情，团体；研究员职位</td><td>a fellowship of scholars<br>学者组成的团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir offered both music and warm fellowship every week.  合唱团每周既提供音乐也带来温暖情谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enmity</td><td>/ˈenməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，仇恨</td><td>long-standing enmity<br>积怨已久的仇恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old enmity between the families still poisons village life.  两家的旧仇至今仍毒害着村里的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>catharsis</b></td><td>/kəˈθɑːrsɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>情绪的宣泄、释放</td><td>emotional catharsis through art<br>借艺术宣泄情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Writing the letter provided a real catharsis for her grief.  写这封信真正宣泄了她的悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cleansing</td><td>/ˈklenzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>清洁，净化；涤除</td><td>a cleansing ritual<br>净化的仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long cry felt like a cleansing of old pain.  那场大哭像是把旧日痛苦涤净了一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 expurgation</del></td><td>/ˌɛkspərˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>删改，删去不当内容</td><td>expurgation of the text<br>对文本的删改</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The censor's expurgation removed three chapters from the novel.  审查者的删改从小说中删去了三章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lustration</td><td>/lʌˈstreɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>净化仪式，洗净</td><td>a rite of lustration<br>一场净化仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancient priests performed lustration before entering the inner temple.  古代祭司进入内殿前要举行净化仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 purgation</td><td>/pərˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>净化，清除；通便</td><td>spiritual purgation<br>精神上的净化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilgrimage was seen as a purgation of past sins.  这次朝圣被视为对往日罪过的净化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 purification</td><td>/ˌpjʊrəfəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>净化，提纯</td><td>water purification system<br>水净化系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village needed a simple method of water purification.  这个村子需要一种简单的净水方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>camouflage</b></td><td>/ˈkæməflɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>伪装，掩饰；伪装物</td><td>military camouflage<br>军事伪装</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers used branches and mud as camouflage in the forest.  士兵们用树枝和泥浆在森林里作伪装。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 costume</td><td>/ˈkɑːstuːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>服装，戏服，装扮</td><td>wear a clever costume<br>穿上巧妙的装扮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sewed her own costume for the school play.  她为学校的话剧自己缝了戏服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guise</td><td>/ˈɡaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>外观，伪装的样子</td><td>under the guise of<br>以…为幌子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He entered the palace under the guise of a merchant.  他以商人为幌子进入了宫殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloak</td><td>/kloʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遮掩，隐藏</td><td>cloak the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials cloaked the truth behind pages of confusing statistics.  官员用一堆令人困惑的统计数字掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dress up</td><td>/drɛs ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>打扮，装扮；美化</td><td>dress up as a pirate<br>装扮成海盗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children dressed up as ghosts for the school party.  孩子们打扮成鬼怪去参加学校晚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unmask</td><td>/ʌnˈmæsk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，揭穿真面目</td><td>unmask the impostor<br>揭穿那个冒充者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The journalist unmasked the company's secret polluting practices.  记者揭露了这家公司秘密排污的做法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>canard</b></td><td>/kəˈnɑːrd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>谣传，误传</td><td>spread an old canard<br>散播一则老谣传</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old canard that vaccines cause illness still circulates online.  说疫苗致病的老谣传仍在网上流传。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 story</td><td>/ˈstɔːri/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>说法，编造的故事，传言</td><td>make up a story<br>编造一套说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old story about the haunted mill was pure invention.  关于那座鬼磨坊的老传言纯属编造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whisper</td><td>/ˈwɪspər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>低声说，耳语</td><td>whisper in her ear<br>在她耳边低语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She whispered the answer so the teacher would not hear.  她低声说出答案，以免老师听见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>candor</b></td><td>/ˈkændər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>坦率，直言不讳</td><td>speak with candor<br>坦率地讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I appreciated her candor about the risks of the operation.  我很欣赏她坦率说明手术的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bluntness</td><td>/ˈblʌntnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>直率，不客气；钝</td><td>refreshing bluntness<br>令人痛快的直率</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bluntness offended some listeners but earned lasting respect.  他的直率得罪了一些听众，却赢得长久的尊重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forthrightness</td><td>/ˈfɔːrθraɪtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>直率，坦诚</td><td>admired for her forthrightness<br>因坦率受人钦佩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her forthrightness made negotiations faster and far less painful.  她的坦率让谈判更快，也少了许多痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frankness</td><td>/ˈfræŋknəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>坦白，直率</td><td>answer with frankness<br>坦率地回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I appreciated the frankness of his letter about the problem.  我很欣赏他在信中谈这个问题时的坦率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unreservedness</del></td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈzɜrvdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>毫无保留，直言不讳</td><td>speak with unreservedness<br>毫无保留地表达</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unreservedness of her praise surprised the whole class.  她毫无保留的赞美让全班都吃惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 straightforwardness</td><td>/ˌstreɪtˈfɔrwərdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>坦率，直截了当</td><td>valued for his straightforwardness<br>因直率而受重视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her straightforwardness saved the committee hours of pointless debate.  她的直率为委员会省下数小时无谓的争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 artifice</td><td>/ˈɑːrtəfɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，欺骗；巧妙手段</td><td>use clever artifice<br>使用巧妙的诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her apparent shyness was pure artifice designed to disarm her rivals.  她表面的羞怯纯属伪装，为的是麻痹对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mendacity</td><td>/menˈdæsəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谎言，虚假，不诚实</td><td>shameless mendacity<br>无耻的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters grew tired of the candidate's constant mendacity.  选民厌倦了这位候选人不断的谎言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissembling</td><td>/dɪˈsemblɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>掩饰真情，假装</td><td>tired of his dissembling<br>厌倦他的掩饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After months of dissembling he finally admitted the truth.  掩饰了数月之后，他终于承认了真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissimulation</td><td>/dɪˌsɪmjəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>掩饰，假装</td><td>a life of dissimulation<br>充满掩饰的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Court life demanded constant dissimulation from every ambitious young noble.  宫廷生活要求每个有野心的年轻贵族不断掩饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indirection</td><td>/ˌɪndəˈrɛkʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迂回，不直截；欺瞒</td><td>answer by indirection<br>以迂回方式回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He learned the news by indirection rather than direct question.  他是通过迂回而非直接询问得知这消息的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>canon</b></td><td>/ˈkænən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>准则，标准；真经，正典</td><td>the literary canon<br>文学正典</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That novel has finally entered the canon of American literature.  那部小说终于进入了美国文学正典。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dogma</td><td>/ˈdɑːɡmə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>教条，信条</td><td>religious dogma<br>宗教教条</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to accept the party's dogma without question.  她拒绝毫不质疑地接受该党的教条。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adore</td><td>/əˈdɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>热爱，极喜爱；崇拜</td><td>absolutely adore someone<br>非常喜爱某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children adore their new puppy.  孩子们非常喜爱他们的新小狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 adulate</del></td><td>/ˈædʒəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>极度谄媚，拍马屁</td><td>adulate a celebrity<br>对名人极力吹捧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters adulated the young singer after her first hit.  记者们在这位年轻歌手第一首热门歌后对她大加吹捧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 idolize</td><td>/ˈaɪdəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>崇拜，把…当偶像</td><td>idolize a famous singer<br>把名歌手当偶像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young players idolize the captain of the national team.  年轻球员把国家队队长当作偶像崇拜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deify</td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worship</td><td>/ˈwɜːrʃəp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>崇拜，敬奉；礼拜</td><td>worship ancient gods<br>敬奉古代神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They gathered at dawn to worship in the small chapel.  他们黎明时聚在小教堂做礼拜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apocrypha</td><td>/əˈpɑkrəfə/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>伪经，来源可疑的作品</td><td>biblical apocrypha<br>圣经伪经</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scholars debate whether the apocrypha belong in the official Bible.  学者争论伪经是否应收入正式圣经。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heterodox</td><td>/ˈhetərədɑːks/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非正统的，异端的</td><td>heterodox opinions<br>非正统的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her heterodox economic theories were ignored for decades before gaining respect.  她非正统的经济理论被忽视数十年后才获得尊重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nontraditional</td><td>/ˌnɑːntrəˈdɪʃənəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非传统的，不循常规的</td><td>a nontraditional approach<br>非传统的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school welcomes nontraditional students who return after working.  这所学校欢迎工作后返校的非传统学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humiliate</td><td>/hjuːˈmɪlieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>羞辱，使丧失尊严</td><td>humiliate him in public<br>当众羞辱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never humiliated a student for asking a simple question.  这位老师从不因学生问简单的问题而羞辱他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
